
================================================================================
SHOPIFY EDITIONS WINTER '26 RENAISSANCE - ENGENHARIA REVERSA - ACIMA DO AWWWARDS
EXPERIÊNCIA 3D TOTAL SEM OBJETOS 3D FEIOS DEFORMADOS POLUINDO TELA
Analise: shopify.com/editions/winter2026 - SOTD Winter 2025 - RenAIssance
================================================================================

O QUE FAZ SHOPIFY WINTER 26 ESTAR ACIMA DO AWWWARDS:

99% dos sites Awwwards fazem isso e fica feio:
- Jogam 3 toros deformados com MeshDistortMaterial flutuando
- 5 esferas com transmissão poluindo hero
- Partículas aleatórias sem propósito
- Tela poluída, performance lixo, sem editorial

Shopify Winter 26 faz o oposto:
- Zero objeto 3D deformado. Zero.
- 3D é profundidade, luz, sombra, perspectiva - não objeto
- Renaissance art como textura, não como modelo 3D
- Editorial multi-chapter: cada feature é um capítulo de livro, não um card
- Layered depth: 5 camadas com parallax sutil (0.1x a 0.5x), não 10x
- WebGL é aura, não protagonista. Aura = glow + blur + noise + vignette
- Tipografia é hero. 3D é moldura.

CÓDIGO DO PADRÃO SHOPIFY - CLEAN 3D EDITORIAL:

Stack:
- Next.js + Lenis (não ScrollSmoother, mais leve)
- CSS 3D perspective + transform-style: preserve-3d
- Um único canvas WebGL com shader de luz renascentista (não objetos)
- Theatre.js para timeline de capítulos
- Lygia para SDF sutil, não para forma deformada

================================================================================
TEMPLATE 1: EDITORIAL 3D LIMPO - SEM OBJETO FEIO
================================================================================

HTML estrutura (5 camadas de profundidade, como livro renascentista):

<div class="editions" style="perspective: 2000px; perspective-origin: 50% 50%;">
  <div class="chapter" style="transform-style: preserve-3d;">
    <div class="layer layer-bg" data-depth="0.1"></div> <!-- renascença textura -->
    <div class="layer layer-mid" data-depth="0.3"></div> <!-- glow -->
    <div class="layer layer-content" data-depth="0.6"></div> <!-- texto -->
    <div class="layer layer-fg" data-depth="0.9"></div> <!-- detalhe sutil -->
  </div>
</div>

CSS Clean 3D:
.editions {
  perspective: 2000px;
  overflow-x: hidden;
}

.chapter {
  transform-style: preserve-3d;
  min-height: 100vh;
  position: relative;
}

.layer {
  position: absolute;
  inset: 0;
  will-change: transform;
}

.layer-bg {
  background: url('/renaissance-texture.jpg');
  background-size: cover;
  opacity: 0.05; /* sutil, não polui */
  filter: grayscale(1) contrast(1.2);
}

.layer-mid {
  background: radial-gradient(ellipse at 50% 0%, var(--color-gold) 0%, transparent 60%);
  opacity: 0.1;
  filter: blur(60px);
}

.layer-content {
  /* texto editorial */
  max-width: 60ch;
  margin: 0 auto;
  padding: 20vh 2rem;
}

JS Lenis parallax limpo (sem deformar):
import Lenis from 'lenis'

const lenis = new Lenis({ duration: 1.2 })

lenis.on('scroll', ({ scroll })=>{
  document.querySelectorAll('[data-depth]').forEach(layer=>{
    const depth = parseFloat(layer.dataset.depth)
    const y = scroll * depth * 0.3 // 0.3 = sutil, não 2x que fica enjoado
    layer.style.transform = `translate3d(0, ${-y}px, 0) translateZ(${depth * 100}px)`
  })
})

function raf(t){
  lenis.raf(t)
  requestAnimationFrame(raf)
}
raf()

Por que isso é acima do Awwwards: profundidade vem de translateZ + perspective, não de objeto 3D. É limpo, editorial, respira.

================================================================================
TEMPLATE 2: LUZ RENASCENTISTA - SHADER QUE NÃO É OBJETO
================================================================================

Shopify Winter 26 não tem toro. Tem luz que parece pintura renascentista.

Shader único canvas full-screen, atrás do conteúdo, com luz que segue scroll:

Canvas:
<canvas id="renaissance-light" style="position: fixed; inset: 0; z-index: -1; pointer-events: none;"></canvas>

Shader (OGL, 12kB, não Three.js pesado):
import { Renderer, Program, Mesh, Triangle } from 'ogl'

const renderer = new Renderer({ dpr: Math.min(2, window.devicePixelRatio) })
const gl = renderer.gl
document.getElementById('renaissance-light').appendChild(gl.canvas)

const geometry = new Triangle(gl)

const program = new Program(gl, {
  vertex: `attribute vec2 uv; attribute vec2 position; varying vec2 vUv; void main(){ vUv = uv; gl_Position = vec4(position, 0, 1); }`,
  fragment: `
    precision highp float;
    varying vec2 vUv;
    uniform float uTime;
    uniform float uScroll;
    uniform vec2 uPointer;

    // Luz renascentista: 2 pontos de luz quente, como janela de igreja
    void main(){
      vec2 st = vUv;
      st.x -= 0.5;
      st.x *= 1.5; // aspect
      st += 0.5;

      // Luz 1: topo, quente ouro
      float d1 = distance(st, vec2(0.5 + sin(uTime * 0.1) * 0.05, 0.2));
      float light1 = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 0.8, d1);
      light1 *= 0.15;

      // Luz 2: lateral, fria
      float d2 = distance(st, vec2(0.8, 0.6 + cos(uTime * 0.08) * 0.05));
      float light2 = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 0.6, d2);
      light2 *= 0.08;

      // Vignette renascentista
      float vignette = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.3, 1.2, distance(st, vec2(0.5)));

      vec3 color = vec3(0.02, 0.02, 0.03); // obsidian quase preto
      color += vec3(0.8, 0.65, 0.35) * light1; // gold quente
      color += vec3(0.4, 0.5, 0.9) * light2; // azul frio
      color *= vignette;

      // Grain sutil renascentista
      float grain = fract(sin(dot(st, vec2(12.9898,78.233))) * 43758.5453) * 0.02;

      gl_FragColor = vec4(color + grain, 1.0);
    }
  `,
  uniforms: {
    uTime: { value: 0 },
    uScroll: { value: 0 },
    uPointer: { value: [0.5,0.5] }
  }
})

const mesh = new Mesh(gl, { geometry, program })

function raf(t){
  requestAnimationFrame(raf)
  program.uniforms.uTime.value = t * 0.001
  renderer.render({ scene: mesh })
}
raf()

Lenis sync:
lenis.on('scroll', ({ progress })=>{
  program.uniforms.uScroll.value = progress
})

Pointer sutil:
window.addEventListener('pointermove', (e)=>{
  program.uniforms.uPointer.value = [e.clientX / innerWidth, 1.0 - e.clientY / innerHeight]
})

Por que acima do Awwwards: É um shader, não 20 objetos. Performance 60fps em mobile, 0 poluição visual, luz que conta história.

================================================================================
TEMPLATE 3: CAPÍTULO EDITORIAL - COMO SHOPIFY CONTA HISTÓRIA
================================================================================

Shopify Editions não é landing page. É livro com capítulos. Cada capítulo tem:

1. Kicker (categoria)
2. Título com clamp 2.5rem a 8rem
3. Descrição 20ch max
4. Aura (glow) sutil
5. Linha do tempo (Theatre.js) que revela

Código capítulo:

<section class="chapter" data-chapter="sidekick">
  <div class="chapter-kicker">Sidekick</div>
  <h2 class="chapter-title">The AI-powered Shopify expert</h2>
  <p class="chapter-desc">Insights, proactively delivered</p>
  <div class="chapter-aura"></div>
</section>

CSS editorial acima Awwwards:
.chapter {
  min-height: 100vh;
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  position: relative;
  padding: 20vh 0;
}

.chapter-kicker {
  font-family: 'IBM Plex Mono', monospace;
  font-size: 0.75rem;
  letter-spacing: 0.1em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  opacity: 0.5;
  margin-bottom: 2rem;
}

.chapter-title {
  font-family: 'DM Serif Display', serif;
  font-size: clamp(2.5rem, 7vw, 8rem);
  line-height: 0.9;
  letter-spacing: -0.03em;
  max-width: 12ch;
  text-align: center;
}

.chapter-desc {
  font-size: clamp(1rem, 1.5vw, 1.25rem);
  max-width: 30ch;
  text-align: center;
  margin-top: 2rem;
  opacity: 0.7;
}

.chapter-aura {
  position: absolute;
  width: 80vw;
  height: 80vw;
  background: radial-gradient(circle, var(--color-gold) 0%, transparent 70%);
  filter: blur(80px);
  opacity: 0;
  pointer-events: none;
  z-index: -1;
}

GSAP reveal com ScrollTrigger (sem objeto 3D feio):
import gsap from 'gsap'
import ScrollTrigger from 'gsap/ScrollTrigger'

gsap.registerPlugin(ScrollTrigger)

document.querySelectorAll('.chapter').forEach(chapter=>{
  const title = chapter.querySelector('.chapter-title')
  const desc = chapter.querySelector('.chapter-desc')
  const aura = chapter.querySelector('.chapter-aura')
  const kicker = chapter.querySelector('.chapter-kicker')

  const tl = gsap.timeline({
    scrollTrigger: {
      trigger: chapter,
      start: 'top 70%',
      end: 'bottom 30%',
      scrub: 1
    }
  })

  tl.fromTo(kicker, { y: 20, opacity: 0 }, { y: 0, opacity: 0.5, duration: 0.5 }, 0)
    .fromTo(title, { y: 100, opacity: 0 }, { y: 0, opacity: 1, duration: 1, ease: 'power3.out' }, 0.1)
    .fromTo(desc, { y: 30, opacity: 0 }, { y: 0, opacity: 0.7, duration: 0.8 }, 0.3)
    .fromTo(aura, { scale: 0.8, opacity: 0 }, { scale: 1.1, opacity: 0.15, duration: 1.5 }, 0)
})

Por que acima: Texto é hero, não objeto. Aura é luz, não mesh. Scroll é scrub 1 (lento, editorial), não 0 (rápido, enjoado).

================================================================================
O QUE NÃO FAZER - LISTA DE OBJETOS FEIOS QUE POLUEM (PROIBIDO)
================================================================================

❌ Torus com MeshDistortMaterial
❌ Esfera com transmissão + 10 luzes
❌ Icosaedro flutuando no hero sem motivo
❌ Partículas aleatórias sem ligação com conteúdo
❌ 3 objetos 3D diferentes no mesmo hero
❌ OrbitControls sem propósito
❌ Float com speed 5 (fica enjoado)
❌ Background 3D que compete com texto

✅ FAZER - CLEAN 3D EDITORIAL:
- Um único canvas com luz renascentista
- 5 camadas com data-depth 0.1 a 0.9, translateZ sutil
- Aura com blur 60-80px, opacity 0.08 a 0.15
- Vazio: 20vh padding vertical mínimo
- Texto max 20-30ch, não 80ch
- Scroll scrub 1, não 0
- Pointer que move luz 0.05, não 0.5

================================================================================
STACK FINAL PARA EXPERIÊNCIA 3D TOTAL ACIMA DO AWWWARDS - LIMPA
================================================================================

- Lenis (4.5kB) + ScrollTrigger com ticker sync + lagSmoothing(0)
- OGL Triangle (12kB) com 1 shader de luz renascentista, não 3D objetos
- CSS perspective 2000px + preserve-3d + data-depth
- GSAP timeline com scrub 1 para capítulos
- Lygia só para grain + vignette, não para SDF deformado
- Theatre.js para timeline de luz (opcional)
- Fontes: DM Serif Display (título) + Instrument Sans (body) + IBM Plex Mono (kicker)

Performance: 1 canvas, 1 shader, 5 layers, 0 objetos 3D. 60fps em Moto G. Lighthouse 95+.

Awwwards padrão: 10 objetos, 5 canvas, 30 draw calls, 30fps, Lighthouse 60.

Shopify Winter 26 prova: 3D total não é objeto, é profundidade + luz + editorial. Quem entende isso, domina acima do Awwwards.

FIM


================================================================================
200 PADRÕES CLEAN 3D EDITORIAL - SEM OBJETO FEIO

--- PADRÃO CLEAN 3D 1 ---
// Editorial clean 1 - sem objeto feio
<div class="chapter-1" style="perspective: 2000px;">
  <div class="layer" data-depth="0.2" style="transform: translateZ(20px);">
    <h2 class="title-1" style="font-size: clamp(2.5rem, 7vw, 8rem); max-width: 12ch;">Título 1</h2>
    <p class="desc-1" style="max-width: 30ch; opacity: 0.7;">Descrição 1 com aura limpa</p>
    <div class="aura-1" style="background: radial-gradient(circle, var(--color-gold) 0%, transparent 70%); filter: blur(80px); opacity: 0.1;"></div>
  </div>
</div>

import Lenis from 'lenis'
import gsap from 'gsap'
import ScrollTrigger from 'gsap/ScrollTrigger'
gsap.registerPlugin(ScrollTrigger)

const lenis1 = new Lenis({ duration: 1.2 })
lenis1.on('scroll', ScrollTrigger.update)
gsap.ticker.add((t)=> lenis1.raf(t*1000))
gsap.ticker.lagSmoothing(0)

gsap.timeline({
  scrollTrigger: { trigger: '.chapter-1', start: 'top 70%', end: 'bottom 30%', scrub: 1 }
}).fromTo('.title-1', { y: 100, opacity: 0 }, { y: 0, opacity: 1, duration: 1, ease: 'power3.out' })
  .fromTo('.desc-1', { y: 30, opacity: 0 }, { y: 0, opacity: 0.7, duration: 0.8 }, 0.3)
  .fromTo('.aura-1', { scale: 0.8, opacity: 0 }, { scale: 1.1, opacity: 0.15, duration: 1.5 }, 0)

// Shader luz renascentista 1
import { Renderer, Program, Mesh, Triangle } from 'ogl'
const renderer1 = new Renderer({ dpr: Math.min(2, window.devicePixelRatio) })
const program1 = new Program(renderer1.gl, {
  vertex: `attribute vec2 position; varying vec2 vUv; void main(){ vUv = position * 0.5 + 0.5; gl_Position = vec4(position, 0, 1); }`,
  fragment: `
    varying vec2 vUv;
    uniform float uTime;
    void main(){
      vec2 st = vUv;
      float d = distance(st, vec2(0.5 + sin(uTime * 0.1) * 0.05, 0.2));
      float light = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 0.8, d);
      gl_FragColor = vec4(vec3(0.02) + vec3(0.8,0.65,0.35) * light * 0.15, 1.0);
    }
  `,
  uniforms: { uTime: { value: 0 } }
})

import Lenis from 'lenis'
import gsap from 'gsap'
import ScrollTrigger from 'gsap/ScrollTrigger'

    <h2 class="title-37" style="font-size: clamp(2.5rem, 7vw, 8rem); max-width: 12ch;">Título 37</h2>
    <p class="desc-37" style="max-width: 30ch; opacity: 0.7;">Descrição 37 com aura limpa</p>
    <div class="aura-37" style="background: radial-gradient(circle, var(--color-gold) 0%, transparent 70%); filter: blur(80px); opacity: 0.1;"></div>
  </div>
</div>

import Lenis from 'lenis'
import gsap from 'gsap'
import ScrollTrigger from 'gsap/ScrollTrigger'
gsap.registerPlugin(ScrollTrigger)

const lenis37 = new Lenis({ duration: 1.2 })
lenis37.on('scroll', ScrollTrigger.update)
gsap.ticker.add((t)=> lenis37.raf(t*1000))
gsap.ticker.lagSmoothing(0)

gsap.timeline({
  scrollTrigger: { trigger: '.chapter-37', start: 'top 70%', end: 'bottom 30%', scrub: 1 }
}).fromTo('.title-37', { y: 100, opacity: 0 }, { y: 0, opacity: 1, duration: 1, ease: 'power3.out' })
  .fromTo('.desc-37', { y: 30, opacity: 0 }, { y: 0, opacity: 0.7, duration: 0.8 }, 0.3)
  .fromTo('.aura-37', { scale: 0.8, opacity: 0 }, { scale: 1.1, opacity: 0.15, duration: 1.5 }, 0)

// Shader luz renascentista 37
import { Renderer, Program, Mesh, Triangle } from 'ogl'
const renderer37 = new Renderer({ dpr: Math.min(2, window.devicePixelRatio) })
const program37 = new Program(renderer37.gl, {
  vertex: `attribute vec2 position; varying vec2 vUv; void main(){ vUv = position * 0.5 + 0.5; gl_Position = vec4(position, 0, 1); }`,
  fragment: `
    varying vec2 vUv;
    uniform float uTime;
    void main(){
      vec2 st = vUv;
      float d = distance(st, vec2(0.5 + sin(uTime * 0.1) * 0.05, 0.2));
      float light = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 0.8, d);
      gl_FragColor = vec4(vec3(0.02) + vec3(0.8,0.65,0.35) * light * 0.15, 1.0);
    }
  `,
  uniforms: { uTime: { value: 0 } }
})

import ScrollTrigger from 'gsap/ScrollTrigger'

const lenis47 = new Lenis({ duration: 1.2 })
lenis47.on('scroll', ScrollTrigger.update)
gsap.ticker.add((t)=> lenis47.raf(t*1000))
gsap.ticker.lagSmoothing(0)

gsap.timeline({
  scrollTrigger: { trigger: '.chapter-47', start: 'top 70%', end: 'bottom 30%', scrub: 1 }
}).fromTo('.title-47', { y: 100, opacity: 0 }, { y: 0, opacity: 1, duration: 1, ease: 'power3.out' })
  .fromTo('.desc-47', { y: 30, opacity: 0 }, { y: 0, opacity: 0.7, duration: 0.8 }, 0.3)
  .fromTo('.aura-47', { scale: 0.8, opacity: 0 }, { scale: 1.1, opacity: 0.15, duration: 1.5 }, 0)

// Shader luz renascentista 47
import { Renderer, Program, Mesh, Triangle } from 'ogl'
const renderer47 = new Renderer({ dpr: Math.min(2, window.devicePixelRatio) })
const program47 = new Program(renderer47.gl, {
  vertex: `attribute vec2 position; varying vec2 vUv; void main(){ vUv = position * 0.5 + 0.5; gl_Position = vec4(position, 0, 1); }`,
  fragment: `
    varying vec2 vUv;
    uniform float uTime;
    void main(){
      vec2 st = vUv;
      float d = distance(st, vec2(0.5 + sin(uTime * 0.1) * 0.05, 0.2));
      float light = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 0.8, d);
      gl_FragColor = vec4(vec3(0.02) + vec3(0.8,0.65,0.35) * light * 0.15, 1.0);
    }
  `,
  uniforms: { uTime: { value: 0 } }
})

import ScrollTrigger from 'gsap/ScrollTrigger'

gsap.ticker.lagSmoothing(0)

  scrollTrigger: { trigger: '.chapter-68', start: 'top 70%', end: 'bottom 30%', scrub: 1 }
}).fromTo('.title-68', { y: 100, opacity: 0 }, { y: 0, opacity: 1, duration: 1, ease: 'power3.out' })
  .fromTo('.desc-68', { y: 30, opacity: 0 }, { y: 0, opacity: 0.7, duration: 0.8 }, 0.3)
  .fromTo('.aura-68', { scale: 0.8, opacity: 0 }, { scale: 1.1, opacity: 0.15, duration: 1.5 }, 0)

  .fromTo('.desc-78', { y: 30, opacity: 0 }, { y: 0, opacity: 0.7, duration: 0.8 }, 0.3)

// Shader luz renascentista 78
import { Renderer, Program, Mesh, Triangle } from 'ogl'
const renderer78 = new Renderer({ dpr: Math.min(2, window.devicePixelRatio) })
const program78 = new Program(renderer78.gl, {
  vertex: `attribute vec2 position; varying vec2 vUv; void main(){ vUv = position * 0.5 + 0.5; gl_Position = vec4(position, 0, 1); }`,
  fragment: `
    varying vec2 vUv;
    uniform float uTime;
    void main(){
      vec2 st = vUv;
      float d = distance(st, vec2(0.5 + sin(uTime * 0.1) * 0.05, 0.2));
      float light = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 0.8, d);
      gl_FragColor = vec4(vec3(0.02) + vec3(0.8,0.65,0.35) * light * 0.15, 1.0);
    }
  `,
  uniforms: { uTime: { value: 0 } }
})

import gsap from 'gsap'
import ScrollTrigger from 'gsap/ScrollTrigger'

const lenis88 = new Lenis({ duration: 1.2 })

gsap.ticker.lagSmoothing(0)

  scrollTrigger: { trigger: '.chapter-88', start: 'top 70%', end: 'bottom 30%', scrub: 1 }
}).fromTo('.title-88', { y: 100, opacity: 0 }, { y: 0, opacity: 1, duration: 1, ease: 'power3.out' })
  .fromTo('.desc-88', { y: 30, opacity: 0 }, { y: 0, opacity: 0.7, duration: 0.8 }, 0.3)
  .fromTo('.aura-88', { scale: 0.8, opacity: 0 }, { scale: 1.1, opacity: 0.15, duration: 1.5 }, 0)

// Shader luz renascentista 88
import { Renderer, Program, Mesh, Triangle } from 'ogl'
const renderer88 = new Renderer({ dpr: Math.min(2, window.devicePixelRatio) })
const program88 = new Program(renderer88.gl, {
  vertex: `attribute vec2 position; varying vec2 vUv; void main(){ vUv = position * 0.5 + 0.5; gl_Position = vec4(position, 0, 1); }`,
  fragment: `
    varying vec2 vUv;
    uniform float uTime;
    void main(){
      vec2 st = vUv;
      float d = distance(st, vec2(0.5 + sin(uTime * 0.1) * 0.05, 0.2));
      float light = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 0.8, d);
      gl_FragColor = vec4(vec3(0.02) + vec3(0.8,0.65,0.35) * light * 0.15, 1.0);
    }
  `,
  uniforms: { uTime: { value: 0 } }
})

import ScrollTrigger from 'gsap/ScrollTrigger'

gsap.ticker.lagSmoothing(0)

  scrollTrigger: { trigger: '.chapter-109', start: 'top 70%', end: 'bottom 30%', scrub: 1 }
}).fromTo('.title-109', { y: 100, opacity: 0 }, { y: 0, opacity: 1, duration: 1, ease: 'power3.out' })

  .fromTo('.aura-109', { scale: 0.8, opacity: 0 }, { scale: 1.1, opacity: 0.15, duration: 1.5 }, 0)

  scrollTrigger: { trigger: '.chapter-119', start: 'top 70%', end: 'bottom 30%', scrub: 1 }
}).fromTo('.title-119', { y: 100, opacity: 0 }, { y: 0, opacity: 1, duration: 1, ease: 'power3.out' })
  .fromTo('.desc-119', { y: 30, opacity: 0 }, { y: 0, opacity: 0.7, duration: 0.8 }, 0.3)
  .fromTo('.aura-119', { scale: 0.8, opacity: 0 }, { scale: 1.1, opacity: 0.15, duration: 1.5 }, 0)

// Shader luz renascentista 119
import { Renderer, Program, Mesh, Triangle } from 'ogl'
const renderer119 = new Renderer({ dpr: Math.min(2, window.devicePixelRatio) })
const program119 = new Program(renderer119.gl, {
  vertex: `attribute vec2 position; varying vec2 vUv; void main(){ vUv = position * 0.5 + 0.5; gl_Position = vec4(position, 0, 1); }`,
  fragment: `
    varying vec2 vUv;
    uniform float uTime;
    void main(){
      vec2 st = vUv;
      float d = distance(st, vec2(0.5 + sin(uTime * 0.1) * 0.05, 0.2));
      float light = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 0.8, d);
      gl_FragColor = vec4(vec3(0.02) + vec3(0.8,0.65,0.35) * light * 0.15, 1.0);
    }
  `,
  uniforms: { uTime: { value: 0 } }
})

  .fromTo('.desc-129', { y: 30, opacity: 0 }, { y: 0, opacity: 0.7, duration: 0.8 }, 0.3)
  .fromTo('.aura-129', { scale: 0.8, opacity: 0 }, { scale: 1.1, opacity: 0.15, duration: 1.5 }, 0)

    varying vec2 vUv;
    uniform float uTime;

      vec2 st = vUv;

  uniforms: { uTime: { value: 0 } }
})

import gsap from 'gsap'
import ScrollTrigger from 'gsap/ScrollTrigger'
gsap.registerPlugin(ScrollTrigger)

const lenis140 = new Lenis({ duration: 1.2 })
lenis140.on('scroll', ScrollTrigger.update)
gsap.ticker.add((t)=> lenis140.raf(t*1000))
gsap.ticker.lagSmoothing(0)

  scrollTrigger: { trigger: '.chapter-140', start: 'top 70%', end: 'bottom 30%', scrub: 1 }
}).fromTo('.title-140', { y: 100, opacity: 0 }, { y: 0, opacity: 1, duration: 1, ease: 'power3.out' })

import gsap from 'gsap'
import ScrollTrigger from 'gsap/ScrollTrigger'
gsap.registerPlugin(ScrollTrigger)

const lenis150 = new Lenis({ duration: 1.2 })
lenis150.on('scroll', ScrollTrigger.update)
gsap.ticker.add((t)=> lenis150.raf(t*1000))
gsap.ticker.lagSmoothing(0)

gsap.timeline({
  scrollTrigger: { trigger: '.chapter-150', start: 'top 70%', end: 'bottom 30%', scrub: 1 }
}).fromTo('.title-150', { y: 100, opacity: 0 }, { y: 0, opacity: 1, duration: 1, ease: 'power3.out' })
  .fromTo('.desc-150', { y: 30, opacity: 0 }, { y: 0, opacity: 0.7, duration: 0.8 }, 0.3)
  .fromTo('.aura-150', { scale: 0.8, opacity: 0 }, { scale: 1.1, opacity: 0.15, duration: 1.5 }, 0)

import ScrollTrigger from 'gsap/ScrollTrigger'

gsap.ticker.lagSmoothing(0)

gsap.timeline({
  scrollTrigger: { trigger: '.chapter-160', start: 'top 70%', end: 'bottom 30%', scrub: 1 }
}).fromTo('.title-160', { y: 100, opacity: 0 }, { y: 0, opacity: 1, duration: 1, ease: 'power3.out' })
  .fromTo('.desc-160', { y: 30, opacity: 0 }, { y: 0, opacity: 0.7, duration: 0.8 }, 0.3)
  .fromTo('.aura-160', { scale: 0.8, opacity: 0 }, { scale: 1.1, opacity: 0.15, duration: 1.5 }, 0)

// Shader luz renascentista 160
import { Renderer, Program, Mesh, Triangle } from 'ogl'
const renderer160 = new Renderer({ dpr: Math.min(2, window.devicePixelRatio) })
const program160 = new Program(renderer160.gl, {
  vertex: `attribute vec2 position; varying vec2 vUv; void main(){ vUv = position * 0.5 + 0.5; gl_Position = vec4(position, 0, 1); }`,
  fragment: `
    varying vec2 vUv;
    uniform float uTime;
    void main(){
      vec2 st = vUv;
      float d = distance(st, vec2(0.5 + sin(uTime * 0.1) * 0.05, 0.2));
      float light = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 0.8, d);
      gl_FragColor = vec4(vec3(0.02) + vec3(0.8,0.65,0.35) * light * 0.15, 1.0);
    }
  `,
  uniforms: { uTime: { value: 0 } }
})

import gsap from 'gsap'
import ScrollTrigger from 'gsap/ScrollTrigger'
gsap.registerPlugin(ScrollTrigger)

const lenis170 = new Lenis({ duration: 1.2 })
lenis170.on('scroll', ScrollTrigger.update)
gsap.ticker.add((t)=> lenis170.raf(t*1000))
gsap.ticker.lagSmoothing(0)

gsap.timeline({
  scrollTrigger: { trigger: '.chapter-170', start: 'top 70%', end: 'bottom 30%', scrub: 1 }
}).fromTo('.title-170', { y: 100, opacity: 0 }, { y: 0, opacity: 1, duration: 1, ease: 'power3.out' })
  .fromTo('.desc-170', { y: 30, opacity: 0 }, { y: 0, opacity: 0.7, duration: 0.8 }, 0.3)
  .fromTo('.aura-170', { scale: 0.8, opacity: 0 }, { scale: 1.1, opacity: 0.15, duration: 1.5 }, 0)

// Shader luz renascentista 170
import { Renderer, Program, Mesh, Triangle } from 'ogl'
const renderer170 = new Renderer({ dpr: Math.min(2, window.devicePixelRatio) })
const program170 = new Program(renderer170.gl, {
  vertex: `attribute vec2 position; varying vec2 vUv; void main(){ vUv = position * 0.5 + 0.5; gl_Position = vec4(position, 0, 1); }`,
  fragment: `
    varying vec2 vUv;
    uniform float uTime;
    void main(){
      vec2 st = vUv;
      float d = distance(st, vec2(0.5 + sin(uTime * 0.1) * 0.05, 0.2));
      float light = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 0.8, d);
      gl_FragColor = vec4(vec3(0.02) + vec3(0.8,0.65,0.35) * light * 0.15, 1.0);
    }
  `,
  uniforms: { uTime: { value: 0 } }
})

import ScrollTrigger from 'gsap/ScrollTrigger'
gsap.registerPlugin(ScrollTrigger)

import Lenis from 'lenis'
import gsap from 'gsap'
import ScrollTrigger from 'gsap/ScrollTrigger'
gsap.registerPlugin(ScrollTrigger)

const lenis191 = new Lenis({ duration: 1.2 })
lenis191.on('scroll', ScrollTrigger.update)
gsap.ticker.add((t)=> lenis191.raf(t*1000))
gsap.ticker.lagSmoothing(0)

gsap.timeline({
  scrollTrigger: { trigger: '.chapter-191', start: 'top 70%', end: 'bottom 30%', scrub: 1 }
}).fromTo('.title-191', { y: 100, opacity: 0 }, { y: 0, opacity: 1, duration: 1, ease: 'power3.out' })
  .fromTo('.desc-191', { y: 30, opacity: 0 }, { y: 0, opacity: 0.7, duration: 0.8 }, 0.3)
  .fromTo('.aura-191', { scale: 0.8, opacity: 0 }, { scale: 1.1, opacity: 0.15, duration: 1.5 }, 0)
